+"""Offline integration/regression tests. Run: python _codex/test_v6dirs.py.
+
+Model transport and output paths are patched; every test-time write is confined
+to a TemporaryDirectory. The real run_parallel, parsers, graph readers and join
+renderer are exercised. No subprocess/model transport is invoked.
+"""
+import ast
+import builtins
+import copy
+from contextlib import ExitStack, redirect_stdout, redirect_stderr
+import hashlib
+import io
+import itertools
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import threading
+import unittest
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+import covjudge
+import draft10
+import evo
+import v6dirs
+import v6fix
+
+
+def segment(s, previous=None, revised=False):
+    sec = s["id"]
+    links = ([{"from": f"{previous}-1", "to": f"{sec}-1", "rel": "提供理由"}]
+             if previous else [])
+    # A forward/dangling link must be removed by tidy before delivery.
+    links.append({"from": "S99-1", "to": f"{sec}-1", "rel": "说明"})
+    return {"sec": sec, "relation": "没在想", "items": [
+        {"id": f"{sec}-1", "text": "刚才的犹豫让他再等一会儿。" if revised else "他还想再等一会儿。",
+         "key": "等一会儿", "follows": False, "explains": ["B1"]}],
+        "because": links, "gaps": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "尚未说清"}]}
+
+
+def whole_answer(w, revised=False):
+    segs = draft10.segs(w)
+    # Deliberately reverse model order: assembly must use sec, not list position.
+    return {"segments": list(reversed([segment(s, segs[i - 1]["id"] if i else None, revised)
+                                       for i, s in enumerate(segs)])),
+            "changes": [{"v6": ["R1"], "now": ["S01-1"], "what": "改写",
+                         "why": "退改版" if revised else "第一版"}]}
+
+
+def check_answer(sec, needs_revision=False, radical=False):
+    result = {"items": [{"id": f"{sec}-1", "label": "ok", "why": "符合边界"}], "links": [],
+              "readable": "是", "readable_why": "读得懂", "lost": [],
+              "unexplained": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "已在 gaps 中，应当排除"}], "wordy": []}
+    if radical:
+        result.update(progress="否" if needs_revision else "是", progress_why="需要接上前段")
+    elif needs_revision:
+        result["wordy"] = [{"ids": [f"{sec}-1"], "why": "需要精简"}]
+    return result
+
+
+class FakeModel:
+    def __init__(self, base):
+        self.base = base
+        self.calls = []
+        self.overrides = {}
+        self.lock = threading.Lock()
+        self.on_call = None
+
+    def done(self, rid):
+        return (self.base / rid / "output.txt").exists()
+
+    def output(self, rid):
+        path = self.base / rid / "output.txt"
+        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if path.exists() else None
+
+    def put(self, rid, answer):
+        path = self.base / rid
+        path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        text = answer if isinstance(answer, str) else json.dumps(answer, ensure_ascii=False)
+        (path / "output.txt").write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+        return text
+
+    def answer(self, rid):
+        if rid in self.overrides:
+            return self.overrides[rid]
+        parts = rid.split("_")
+        prefix, w = parts[:2]
+        segs = draft10.segs(w)
+        if prefix in ("v6w", "v6wr"):
+            return whole_answer(w, revised=prefix == "v6wr")
+        if prefix in ("v6k", "v6sk"):
+            sec = parts[2]
+            return check_answer(sec, needs_revision=sec == segs[1]["id"], radical=prefix == "v6sk")
+        if prefix in ("v6s", "v6sr"):
+            sec = parts[2]
+            i = next(i for i, s in enumerate(segs) if s["id"] == sec)
+            return segment(segs[i], segs[i - 1]["id"] if i else None, revised=prefix == "v6sr")
+        if prefix == "v6sc":
+            # Missing the last relation exercises the specified default + error record.
+            return {"center": "犹豫仍然影响着等待。", "why": "贯穿全曲",
+                    "relations": {s["id"]: "还说不清" for s in segs[:-1]}}
+        raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected model call: {rid}")
+
+    def call(self, rid, message, model, meta):
+        with self.lock:
+            if self.done(rid):
+                raise AssertionError(f"Completed run called again: {rid}")
+            self.calls.append({"rid": rid, "message": message, "model": model, "meta": meta})
+        if self.on_call:
+            self.on_call(rid, message)
+        path = self.base / rid
+        path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        (path / "input.txt").write_text(message, encoding="utf-8")
+        (path / "meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta), encoding="utf-8")
+        return self.put(rid, self.answer(rid))
+
+
+class OfflineTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="v6dirs_test_")
+        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
+        self.base = Path(self.temp.name).resolve()
+        self.out = self.base / "evaluations" / "v6fix"
+        self.fake = FakeModel(self.base / "runs")
+        self.stack = ExitStack()
+        self.addCleanup(self.stack.close)
+        self.stdout, self.stderr = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
+        self.stack.enter_context(redirect_stdout(self.stdout))
+        self.stack.enter_context(redirect_stderr(self.stderr))
+        self.writes = []
+        # Both pathlib and builtins writes are guarded, including unchanged join.
+        def guard(original):
+            def opened(file, mode="r", *args, **kwargs):
+                if isinstance(mode, str) and any(c in mode for c in "wax+"):
+                    path = Path(file).resolve()
+                    if not path.is_relative_to(self.base):
+                        raise AssertionError(f"Write outside temporary directory: {path}")
+                    self.writes.append(path)
+                return original(file, mode, *args, **kwargs)
+            return opened
+        self.stack.enter_context(patch("io.open", guard(io.open)))
+        self.stack.enter_context(patch("builtins.open", guard(builtins.open)))
+        for obj, name, value in ((evo, "RUNS", self.fake.base), (evo, "call", self.fake.call),
+                                 (evo, "done", self.fake.done), (evo, "output", self.fake.output),
+                                 (v6fix, "OUT", self.out)):
+            self.stack.enter_context(patch.object(obj, name, value))
+        self.parallel = self.stack.enter_context(patch.object(evo, "run_parallel", wraps=evo.run_parallel))
+
+    def read(self, w, tag):
+        return json.loads((self.out / f"{w}_{tag}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+
+    def assert_graph(self, w, tag, direction):
+        data = self.read(w, tag)
+        graph = v6fix.graph_of(w, tag)
+        self.assertEqual(data["graph"], graph)
+        self.assertEqual([s["sec"] for s in graph["segments"]], [s["id"] for s in draft10.segs(w)])
+        self.assertEqual(list(data["rows"]), [s["id"] for s in draft10.segs(w)])
+        md = (self.out / f"{w}_{tag}.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertEqual(md, f"# Op.{w[2:]}｜关系图（{direction} {tag}）\n\n" + v6fix.view(graph))
+        seen = set()
+        for s in graph["segments"]:
+            here = {x["id"] for x in s["items"]}
+            self.assertTrue(all(x["from"] in seen and x["to"] in here for x in s["because"]))
+            seen |= here
+            self.assertEqual(set(data["rows"][s["sec"]]), {"edit", "check", "notes", "revised"})
+        # The exact assembly used by blind: old/new graph -> view -> BLIND.format.
+        message = v6fix.BLIND.format(NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], WHAT=v6fix.WHAT,
+                                    X=v6fix.view(v6fix.v6(w)), Y=v6fix.view(graph), BEADS=v6fix.beads(w))
+        self.assertIn(graph["center"], message)
+        return data
+
+    def assert_routes(self):
+        for call in self.fake.calls:
+            prefix = call["rid"].split("_")[0]
+            stage = {"v6w": "v6-whole", "v6wr": "v6-whole-rev", "v6k": "v6-check",
+                     "v6s": "v6-rad", "v6sr": "v6-rad-rev", "v6sk": "v6-rad-check",
+                     "v6sc": "v6-rad-center"}[prefix]
+            writing = prefix in ("v6w", "v6wr", "v6s", "v6sr")
+            self.assertEqual(call["meta"], {"stage": stage, **({"web": True} if writing else {})})
+            fallback = call["rid"].endswith("b")
+            expected = evo.G56 if prefix in ("v6k", "v6sk") or fallback else evo.G6
+            self.assertEqual(call["model"], expected)
+
+    def test_whole_full_both_pieces_fallback_revision_cache_and_join(self):
+        self.fake.overrides["v6w_op60_w1"] = "not json"
+        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+            v6dirs.whole(w)
+            data = self.assert_graph(w, "w1", "A2 整首修补")
+            self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["center"], v6fix.v6(w)["center"])
+            self.assertTrue(all(row["revised"] for row in data["rows"].values()))
+            self.assertEqual(data["changes"][0]["why"], "退改版")
+            self.assertEqual(len(data["rows"]["S02"]["notes"]), 1)
+            self.assertEqual(data["rows"]["S01"]["notes"], [])
+            for call in self.fake.calls:
+                if call["rid"].startswith(f"v6k_{w}_"):
+                    sec = call["rid"].split("_")[2]
+                    done = call["message"].split("【前面已经修好的段】\n", 1)[1].split("\n\n【这一段 v6", 1)[0]
+                    n = int(sec[1:])
+                    for i in range(1, len(data["rows"]) + 1):
+                        self.assertEqual(f"### S{i:02d}｜" in done, i < n)
+            rev = next(x for x in self.fake.calls if x["rid"] == f"v6wr_{w}_w1")
+            note_block = rev["message"].split("【上一次的修补被核对者退回的地方】", 1)[1].split("上一次的修补结果：", 1)[0]
+            self.assertIn("### S02", note_block)
+            self.assertNotIn("### S01", note_block)
+        self.assertEqual(self.parallel.call_count, 2)
+        self.assertTrue(all(c.args[1] == 4 for c in self.parallel.call_args_list))
+        joined = v6fix.join("w1").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertIn("肖邦《船歌》Op.60", joined)
+        self.assertIn("肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61", joined)
+        self.assertIn("线没有单独说明的听感", joined)
+        before = len(self.fake.calls)
+        v6dirs.whole("op60")
+        v6dirs.whole("op61")
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.fake.calls), before)
+        self.assert_routes()
+        self.assertTrue(self.writes)
+
+    def test_rad_full_both_pieces_fallback_progress_revision_center_cache_and_join(self):
+        self.fake.overrides["v6s_op60_S01_s1"] = "not json"
+        snapshots = []
+        def inspect(rid, message):
+            if rid.startswith("v6s_") or rid.startswith("v6sc_"):
+                w = rid.split("_")[1]
+                if rid.startswith("v6sc_"):
+                    data = self.read(w, "s1")
+                    self.assertEqual(len(data["graph"]["segments"]), len(draft10.segs(w)))
+                else:
+                    sec = rid.split("_")[2]
+                    n = int(sec[1:])
+                    if n == 1:
+                        return
+                    data = self.read(w, "s1")
+                    self.assertEqual(len(data["graph"]["segments"]), n - 1)
+                    previous = data["graph"]["segments"][-1]
+                    self.assertIn(previous["items"][0]["text"], message)
+                self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["center"], "")
+                self.assertTrue(all(s["relation"] == "" for s in data["graph"]["segments"]))
+                self.assertNotIn("（没在想）", message)
+                self.assertNotIn("（正在面对）", message)
+                snapshots.append(copy.deepcopy(data))
+        self.fake.on_call = inspect
+        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+            with patch.object(v6fix, "v6", side_effect=AssertionError("B1 must not load v6")):
+                v6dirs.rad(w)
+            data = self.assert_graph(w, "s1", "B1 激进逐段")
+            self.assertTrue(data["rows"]["S02"]["revised"])
+            self.assertEqual(data["rows"]["S02"]["notes"], ["- 递进不够：需要接上前段"])
+            self.assertFalse(data["rows"]["S01"]["revised"])
+            self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["segments"][-1]["relation"], "正在面对")
+            self.assertTrue(any("Center 缺少 relation" in x for x in data["errors"]))
+            center_call = next(c for c in self.fake.calls if c["rid"] == f"v6sc_{w}_s1")
+            self.assertNotRegex(center_call["message"], r"\[S\d+-\d+\]")
+            order = [c["rid"] for c in self.fake.calls]
+            self.assertLess(order.index(f"v6sr_{w}_S02_s1"), order.index(f"v6s_{w}_S03_s1"))
+        self.assertEqual(len(snapshots), 27)
+        self.assertIn("犹豫仍然影响着等待。", v6fix.join("s1").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        self.fake.on_call = None
+        before = len(self.fake.calls)
+        v6dirs.rad("op60")
+        v6dirs.rad("op61")
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.fake.calls), before)
+        self.assert_routes()
+
+    def test_whole_no_notes_no_revision(self):
+        for s in draft10.segs("op60"):
+            self.fake.overrides[f"v6k_op60_{s['id']}_clean"] = check_answer(s["id"])
+        v6dirs.whole("op60", "clean")
+        self.assertFalse(any(c["rid"].startswith("v6wr_") for c in self.fake.calls))
+        self.assertFalse(any(r["revised"] for r in self.read("op60", "clean")["rows"].values()))
+
+    def test_whole_missing_segment_revision_failure_preserves_first(self):
+        first = whole_answer("op60")
+        first["segments"] = [s for s in first["segments"] if s["sec"] != "S03"]
+        self.fake.overrides.update(v6w_op60_missing=first, v6wr_op60_missing="broken")
+        v6dirs.whole("op60", "missing")
+        data = self.assert_graph("op60", "missing", "A2 整首修补")
+        self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["segments"][2]["items"], [])
+        self.assertEqual(data["rows"]["S03"]["edit"]["items"], [])
+        self.assertFalse(any(r["revised"] for r in data["rows"].values()))
+        self.assertEqual(data["changes"], first["changes"])
+        self.assertEqual(len(data["errors"]), 2)
+
+    def test_generation_both_parse_failures_stop(self):
+        for direction, rid in (("whole", "v6w_op60_bad"), ("rad", "v6s_op60_S01_bad")):
+            self.fake.overrides.update({rid: "bad", rid + "b": "bad"})
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "G6 和 G56"):
+                getattr(v6dirs, direction)("op60", "bad")
+        self.assertFalse(self.out.exists())
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.fake.calls), 4)
+        self.assert_routes()
+
+    def test_rad_revision_failure_and_center_failure_preserve_progress(self):
+        self.fake.overrides.update(v6sr_op60_S02_failcenter="bad", v6sc_op60_failcenter="bad")
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "Center"):
+            v6dirs.rad("op60", "failcenter")
+        data = self.read("op60", "failcenter")
+        self.assertEqual(len(data["graph"]["segments"]), 14)
+        self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["center"], "")
+        self.assertFalse(data["rows"]["S02"]["revised"])
+        self.assertIn("他还想再等一会儿。", v6fix.view(data["graph"]))
+        self.assertTrue(any("逐段退改解析失败" in x for x in data["errors"]))
+        self.assertTrue((self.out / "op60_failcenter.md").exists())
+
+    def test_check_failure_stops_without_accepting_unchecked_output(self):
+        self.fake.overrides["v6sk_op60_S02_checkfail"] = "bad"
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "核对"):
+            v6dirs.rad("op60", "checkfail")
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.read("op60", "checkfail")["graph"]["segments"]), 1)
+        self.assertFalse(any(c["rid"].startswith("v6sc_") for c in self.fake.calls))
+        self.fake.overrides["v6k_op60_S01_checkfailwhole"] = "bad"
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "核对"):
+            v6dirs.whole("op60", "checkfailwhole")
+        self.assertFalse((self.out / "op60_checkfailwhole.json").exists())
+
+    def test_rad_review_criteria_without_lost(self):
+        k = {"items": [{"id": "S02-1", "label": "越界", "why": "新增事件"}],
+             "links": [{"from": "S01-1", "to": "S02-1", "label": "勉强", "why": "不能推出"}],
+             "readable": "否", "readable_why": "难懂", "lost": [{"v6": ["R1"], "what": "不应出现"}],
+             "unexplained": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "已在 gaps"}, {"bead": "B5", "why": "未解释"}],
+             "wordy": [{"ids": ["S02-1"], "why": "重复"}], "progress": "否", "progress_why": "没有前因"}
+        edit = {"gaps": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "尚未解释"}]}
+        notes = v6dirs.rad_notes(k, {}, edit)
+        expected = v6fix.review_notes({**k, "lost": []}, {}, edit) + ["- 递进不够：没有前因"]
+        self.assertEqual(notes, expected)
+        self.assertEqual(len(notes), 6)
+        self.assertFalse(any("丢了 v6" in n or "B4" in n for n in notes))
+
+    def test_dry_offline_and_placeholders(self):
+        with patch.object(evo, "ROOT", self.base):
+            for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+                messages = v6dirs.dry(w)
+                self.assertEqual(len(messages), 5)
+                for name, message in messages.items():
+                    self.assertEqual(message, (self.base / "_codex" / f"dry_{w}_{name}.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+                    self.assertNotRegex(message, r"\{[A-Z_]+\}")
+                    self.assertNotIn("{{", message)
+                    # Nested JSON objects legitimately end in }}; parse the
+                    # sample instead of treating closing braces as placeholders.
+                    blocks = re.findall(r"```json\s*(.*?)```", message, re.S)
+                    self.assertEqual(len(blocks), 1)
+                    json.loads(blocks[0])
+                self.assertIn(v6dirs.FIRST, messages["rad_first"])
+                self.assertIn("[S01-1]", messages["rad_second"])
+                self.assertNotIn("（正在面对）", messages["rad_second"])
+        self.assertEqual(self.fake.calls, [])
+
+    def test_reserved_tag_and_path_escape_do_not_call_or_write(self):
+        for fn in (v6dirs.whole, v6dirs.rad):
+            for tag in ("r2", "../r2", "..\\r2", "", "a/b"):
+                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                    fn("op60", tag)
+        self.assertEqual(self.fake.calls, [])
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+
+class BaselineTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.baseline = json.loads((ROOT / "_codex" / "v6dirs_baseline.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+
+    def test_protected_files_and_all_v6fix_code_except_view_unchanged(self):
+        for name, digest in self.baseline["sha256"].items():
+            self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256((ROOT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest(), digest, name)
+        source = (ROOT / "v6fix.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        tree = ast.parse(source)
+        stripped = ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if not (isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == "view")], type_ignores=[])
+        self.assertEqual(ast.dump(stripped, include_attributes=False), self.baseline["v6fix_without_view_ast"])
+        original = '            md += [f"### {s[\'sec\']}｜{s[\'bars\']} 小节　（{s.get(\'relation\')}）", ""]'
+        changed = ('            relation = f"　（{s.get(\'relation\')}）" if s.get("relation") else ""\n'
+                   '            md += [f"### {s[\'sec\']}｜{s[\'bars\']} 小节{relation}", ""]')
+        expected = self.baseline["view_source"].replace(original, changed)
+        self.assertNotEqual(expected, self.baseline["view_source"])
+        self.assertEqual(ast.get_source_segment(source, next(n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == "view")), expected)
+
+    def test_both_real_v6_views_exactly_match_before_change(self):
+        namespace = dict(vars(v6fix))
+        exec(self.baseline["view_source"], namespace)
+        old_view = namespace["view"]
+        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+            graph = v6fix.v6(w)
+            self.assertTrue(all(s.get("relation") for s in graph["segments"]))
+            for ids in (False, True):
+                self.assertEqual(v6fix.view(graph, ids=ids), self.baseline["views"][w][str(ids)])
+            for ids, center, head, secs in itertools.product((False, True), (False, True), (False, True), (None, ["S02", "S03"])):
+                opts = dict(ids=ids, center=center, head=head, secs=secs)
+                self.assertEqual(v6fix.view(graph, **opts), old_view(graph, **opts), (w, opts))
+        for relation in (None, ""):
+            graph = {"center": "", "segments": [{"sec": "S01", "bars": "1–3", "relation": relation, "items": [], "because": []}]}
+            self.assertEqual(v6fix.view(graph, center=False), "### S01｜1–3 小节")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

+# v6dirs 实现与验证记录
+
+工作目录：`D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019`。任务日期：2026-10-29。
+
+## 边界与准备
+
+- 只实现 A2/B1 运行器和离线验证；未启动真实 `whole` / `rad`。
+- Python 读写文本全部显式使用 UTF-8。最初尝试 PowerShell here-string 经管道传给 Python 时，PowerShell 5.1 在 stdin 前带了 BOM，触发 SyntaxError；该命令未执行任何文件操作。随后改用 `python -c` 和补丁工具。
+- 已阅读 `v6fix.py`、`v6dirs_prompts.py`、`evo.py` 及分段、作品名与珠子读取接口；工作目录及检查的父目录没有适用的 AGENTS.md。
+- 修改前保存 `_codex/v6dirs_baseline.json`：受保护文件 SHA-256、除 view 外的 v6fix AST、原 view 函数源码、两首 v6 在 ids=False/True 下的原始渲染。
+- op60 有 14 段，op61 有 13 段。
+- 不操作后台进程、watchdog、缓存保温、定时任务。运行器拒绝使用 `r2`，避免写入仍在运行的 A1 结果。
+
+## 实现
+
+- 新增 `v6dirs.py`，提供 `whole <w> [w1]`、`rad <w> [s1]`、`dry <w>`。
+- A2：原始 v6 + 全曲珠子 + 顺序诊断；按 sec 重组；tidy；核对使用独立的前缀图，调用原 `v6fix.check`，`evo.run_parallel(..., 4)`；收集原 `review_notes`，最多一次整首退改；保留最终 edit 和 changes。
+- B1：按段串行；只传已完成段落和本段珠子；首次和退改后均清空 relation；核对沿用 A1 的意见提取规则，仅去掉 lost 并增加 progress；每段立即保存 JSON/MD；最后调用 Center 并填 relation，缺项默认“正在面对”且记录。
+- 首轮生成 JSON/基本容器结构无法解析时，使用 G56 和后缀 b 重试一次；所有调用先检查 `evo.done`。退改失败保留第一版并记录。核对或 Center 无有效解析结果时抛错退出，B1 保留已落盘进度。
+- 所有模型调用仅经 `evo.call`，写作/退改 meta 有 web=True；核对/Center 没有 web。没有额外模型参数、推理参数、槽位、节流、轮询。
+- JSON 保持 graph/rows 接口；A2 另含 changes；异常兜底情况另存 errors。MD 使用指定 A2/B1 标题及 `v6fix.view(graph)`。
+- `v6fix.py` 仅修改 view 的段标题两行：relation 为 None 或空串时省略括号，非空时保留原完整空格、标点和内容。
+- `_codex/test_v6dirs.py` 使用 unittest/monkeypatch；FakeModel 的 call/done/output 仅使用 TemporaryDirectory，另将 evo.RUNS 和 v6fix.OUT 指向临时目录。文件写入守卫同时检查 builtins.open 与 io.open，任何临时目录外写入立即报错。
+
+## 初次 dry
+
+直接运行 `python v6dirs.py dry op60` 和 `python v6dirs.py dry op61`，均成功，输出到 `_codex/dry_{w}_*.txt`。
+
+| 消息 | op60 字符数 | op61 字符数 |
+| --- | ---: | ---: |
+| whole | 51279 | 52314 |
+| rad_first | 2510 | 2813 |
+| rad_second | 2752 | 2904 |
+| rad_check | 1572 | 1717 |
+| rad_center | 460 | 467 |
+
+以上为 Python len，包含空格与换行。whole 的诊断来自现有只读诊断缓存；dry 中 B1 使用明确构造的假段落，不调用模型。
+
+## 验证结果
+
+首轮离线执行：12 项中 11 项通过；唯一失败是测试错误地把 Center 示例中嵌套 JSON 的合法 `}}` 当成未展开花括号。运行器和提示词没有问题。已删除错误的括号尾部断言，保留大写占位符检查与对每份 JSON 示例的 `json.loads` 验证。B1 完整流程额外禁止调用 v6fix.v6，确认运行器不会读取底稿。
+
+待补充修正后的完整测试及最终边界自查。

+"""A2 whole-piece repair and B1 chained generation; prompts owned by v6dirs_prompts.
+
+python v6dirs.py whole <op60|op61> [tag=w1]
+python v6dirs.py rad   <op60|op61> [tag=s1]
+python v6dirs.py dry   <op60|op61>       # no model calls
+"""
+import argparse
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+
+import covjudge
+import draft10
+import evo
+import v6dirs_prompts as prompts
+import v6fix
+
+
+FIRST = "（这是第一段，前面还没有）"
+
+
+def _target(w, tag):
+    if w not in covjudge.NAME:
+        raise ValueError(f"未知作品：{w}")
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]*", tag):
+        raise ValueError("tag 只能包含英文字母、数字、下划线和连字符")
+    if tag == "r2":
+        raise ValueError("r2 属于正在运行的 A1，不能覆盖；请使用独立 tag")
+
+
+def _record(errors, text):
+    errors.append(text)
+    print(f"WARNING: {text}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
+
+
+def _ask(rid, message, model, stage, web=False):
+    meta = {"stage": stage}
+    if web:
+        meta["web"] = True
+    if not evo.done(rid):
+        evo.call(rid, message, model=model, meta=meta)
+    return v6fix.parse(rid)
+
+
+def _whole_ok(result):
+    return isinstance(result, dict) and isinstance(result.get("segments"), list)
+
+
+def _segment_ok(result):
+    return isinstance(result, dict) and isinstance(result.get("items"), list)
+
+
+def _generate(rid, message, stage, valid):
+    for run_id, model in ((rid, evo.G6), (rid + "b", evo.G56)):
+        result = _ask(run_id, message, model, stage, web=True)
+        if valid(result):
+            return result
+    raise RuntimeError(f"{rid}: G6 和 G56 均未返回可解析的结果，停")
+
+
+def whole_message(w, g6=None):
+    if g6 is None:
+        g6 = v6fix.v6(w)
+    diag = "\n\n".join(f"### {s['id']}\n" + v6fix.diag_for(w, s["id"])
+                       for s in draft10.segs(w))
+    return prompts.WHOLE.format(
+        NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], WHAT=v6fix.WHAT, CENTER=g6["center"],
+        V6=v6fix.view(g6, ids=True, center=False), BEADS=v6fix.beads(w), DIAG=diag)
+
+
+def _assemble(w, result, center, errors):
+    by_sec = {x.get("sec"): x for x in result["segments"] if isinstance(x, dict)}
+    graph = {"center": center, "segments": []}
+    edits = {}
+    for s in draft10.segs(w):
+        sec = s["id"]
+        edit = by_sec.get(sec)
+        if edit is None:
+            _record(errors, f"{w} {sec}: 整首输出缺段，使用空 items")
+            edit = {"sec": sec, "items": [], "because": [], "gaps": []}
+        edits[sec] = edit
+        graph["segments"].append(v6fix.seg_graph(edit, sec, s))
+    return v6fix.tidy(graph), edits
+
+
+def _save(w, tag, graph, rows, direction, errors, **extra):
+    v6fix.OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    data = {"graph": graph, "rows": rows, **extra}
+    if errors:
+        data["errors"] = list(errors)
+    (v6fix.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.json").write_text(
+        json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
+    opus = "Op.60" if w == "op60" else "Op.61"
+    (v6fix.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.md").write_text(
+        f"# {opus}｜关系图（{direction} {tag}）\n\n" + v6fix.view(graph), encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def whole(w, tag="w1"):
+    _target(w, tag)
+    g6 = v6fix.v6(w)
+    message = whole_message(w, g6)
+    errors = []
+    result = _generate(f"v6w_{w}_{tag}", message, "v6-whole", _whole_ok)
+    graph, edits = _assemble(w, result, g6["center"], errors)
+    jobs = []
+    for i, new in enumerate(graph["segments"]):
+        sec = new["sec"]
+        done = {"center": g6["center"], "segments": graph["segments"][:i]}
+        cur = {"center": g6["center"], "segments": [x for x in g6["segments"] if x["sec"] == sec]}
+        jobs.append((sec, lambda sec=sec, done=done, cur=cur, new=new:
+                     v6fix.check(w, sec, tag, done, cur, new)))
+    checks = evo.run_parallel(jobs, 4)
+    rows = {}
+    for new in graph["segments"]:
+        sec = new["sec"]
+        k = checks.get(sec)
+        if not isinstance(k, dict) or not k:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"{w} {sec}: 核对未返回可解析的结果，停")
+        rows[sec] = {"edit": edits[sec], "check": k,
+                     "notes": v6fix.review_notes(k, new, edits[sec]), "revised": False}
+    if any(row["notes"] for row in rows.values()):
+        notes = "\n\n".join(f"### {sec}\n" + "\n".join(row["notes"])
+                            for sec, row in rows.items() if row["notes"])
+        revision = _ask(
+            f"v6wr_{w}_{tag}", message + prompts.WHOLE_REV.format(
+                NOTES=notes, PREV=v6fix.view(graph, ids=True, center=False)),
+            evo.G6, "v6-whole-rev", web=True)
+        if _whole_ok(revision):
+            result = revision
+            graph, edits = _assemble(w, result, g6["center"], errors)
+            for sec, row in rows.items():
+                row.update(edit=edits[sec], revised=True)
+        else:
+            _record(errors, f"{w}: 整首退改解析失败，保留第一版")
+    _save(w, tag, graph, rows, "A2 整首修补", errors, changes=result.get("changes") or [])
+    old = {x["sec"]: x for x in g6["segments"]}
+    for new in graph["segments"]:
+        sec = new["sec"]
+        row = rows[sec]
+        print(f"{w} {sec}: v6 {len(old[sec]['items'])} 条 → {len(new['items'])} 条"
+              f" | 意见 {len(row['notes'])} | 退改 {row['revised']}", flush=True)
+    return graph
+
+
+def _done(graph):
+    return v6fix.view(graph, ids=True, center=False) if graph["segments"] else FIRST
+
+
+def rad_message(w, s, graph):
+    segs = "\n".join(f"{x['id']}｜{x['start']}–{x['end']} 小节" for x in draft10.segs(w))
+    return prompts.RAD.format(NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], SEC=s["id"], A=s["start"], B=s["end"],
+                              WHAT=prompts.WHAT_RAD, SEGS=segs, DONE=_done(graph),
+                              BEADS=v6fix.beads(w, s["id"]))
+
+
+def rad_check_message(w, sec, graph, new):
+    return prompts.RAD_CHK.format(
+        DONE=_done(graph), NEW=v6fix.view({"center": "", "segments": [new]}, ids=True, center=False)
+        + v6fix._links(new), BEADS=v6fix.beads(w, sec))
+
+
+def rad_center_message(w, graph):
+    return prompts.RAD_CENTER.format(
+        NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], LINE=v6fix.view(graph, ids=False, center=False))
+
+
+def rad_notes(k, new, edit):
+    # Reuse exactly the A1 criteria, except B1 has progression instead of lost-v6.
+    notes = v6fix.review_notes({key: value for key, value in k.items() if key != "lost"}, new, edit)
+    if k.get("progress") == "否":
+        notes.append(f"- 递进不够：{k.get('progress_why', '')}")
+    return notes
+
+
+def _rad_segment(edit, s):
+    new = v6fix.seg_graph(edit, s["id"], s)
+    new["relation"] = ""
+    return new
+
+
+def rad(w, tag="s1"):
+    _target(w, tag)
+    graph = {"center": "", "segments": []}
+    rows, errors = {}, []
+    for s in draft10.segs(w):
+        sec = s["id"]
+        message = rad_message(w, s, graph)
+        edit = _generate(f"v6s_{w}_{sec}_{tag}", message, "v6-rad", _segment_ok)
+        new = _rad_segment(edit, s)
+        k = _ask(f"v6sk_{w}_{sec}_{tag}", rad_check_message(w, sec, graph, new),
+                 evo.G56, "v6-rad-check")
+        if not isinstance(k, dict) or not k:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"{w} {sec}: 核对未返回可解析的结果，停；已完成的段保留在进度文件")
+        notes = rad_notes(k, new, edit)
+        revised = False
+        if notes:
+            prev = v6fix.view({"center": "", "segments": [new]}, ids=True, center=False)
+            revision = _ask(f"v6sr_{w}_{sec}_{tag}",
+                            message + prompts.RAD_REV.format(NOTES="\n".join(notes), PREV=prev),
+                            evo.G6, "v6-rad-rev", web=True)
+            if _segment_ok(revision):
+                edit, new, revised = revision, _rad_segment(revision, s), True
+            else:
+                _record(errors, f"{w} {sec}: 逐段退改解析失败，保留第一版")
+        graph["segments"].append(new)
+        v6fix.tidy(graph)
+        rows[sec] = {"edit": edit, "check": k, "notes": notes, "revised": revised}
+        _save(w, tag, graph, rows, "B1 激进逐段", errors)
+        print(f"{w} {sec}: {len(new['items'])} 条 | 意见 {len(notes)} | 退改 {revised}"
+              f" | gaps {[x.get('bead') for x in edit.get('gaps') or []]}", flush=True)
+    result = _ask(f"v6sc_{w}_{tag}", rad_center_message(w, graph), evo.G6, "v6-rad-center")
+    if not (isinstance(result, dict) and isinstance(result.get("center"), str)
+            and result["center"].strip() and isinstance(result.get("relations"), dict)):
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{w}: Center 未返回可解析的结果，停；逐段进度已保存")
+    graph["center"] = result["center"]
+    for seg in graph["segments"]:
+        sec = seg["sec"]
+        relation = result["relations"].get(sec)
+        if not relation:
+            relation = "正在面对"
+            _record(errors, f"{w} {sec}: Center 缺少 relation，填入正在面对")
+        seg["relation"] = relation
+    _save(w, tag, graph, rows, "B1 激进逐段", errors)
+    return graph
+
+
+def dry(w):
+    segs = draft10.segs(w)
+    graph = {"center": "", "segments": []}
+    first = _rad_segment({"items": [{"id": f"{segs[0]['id']}-1", "text": "他还说不清自己的感受。",
+                                    "key": "说不清", "explains": ["B1"]}], "because": [], "gaps": []}, segs[0])
+    fake = {"center": "", "segments": [first]}
+    second = _rad_segment({"items": [{"id": f"{segs[1]['id']}-1", "text": "刚才的犹豫让他多想了一会儿。",
+                                     "key": "犹豫", "explains": ["B1"]}],
+                           "because": [{"from": first["items"][0]["id"], "to": f"{segs[1]['id']}-1",
+                                        "rel": "提供理由"}]}, segs[1])
+    messages = {"whole": whole_message(w), "rad_first": rad_message(w, segs[0], graph),
+                "rad_second": rad_message(w, segs[1], fake),
+                "rad_check": rad_check_message(w, segs[1]["id"], fake, second),
+                "rad_center": rad_center_message(w, {"center": "", "segments": [first, second]})}
+    out = evo.ROOT / "_codex"
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for name, message in messages.items():
+        path = out / f"dry_{w}_{name}.txt"
+        path.write_text(message, encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"{path.name}: {len(message)} 字符", flush=True)
+    return messages
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    parser.add_argument("direction", choices=("whole", "rad", "dry"))
+    parser.add_argument("work", choices=("op60", "op61"))
+    parser.add_argument("tag", nargs="?")
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        if args.direction == "dry":
+            if args.tag is not None:
+                parser.error("dry 不接受 tag")
+            dry(args.work)
+        elif args.direction == "whole":
+            whole(args.work, args.tag or "w1")
+        else:
+            rad(args.work, args.tag or "s1")
+    except (RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
+        parser.exit(1, f"ERROR: {exc}\n")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""Offline integration/regression tests. Run: python _codex/test_v6dirs.py.
+
+Model transport and output paths are patched; every test-time write is confined
+to a TemporaryDirectory. The real run_parallel, parsers, graph readers and join
+renderer are exercised. No subprocess/model transport is invoked.
+"""
+import ast
+import builtins
+import copy
+from contextlib import ExitStack, redirect_stdout, redirect_stderr
+import hashlib
+import io
+import itertools
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import threading
+import unittest
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+import covjudge
+import draft10
+import evo
+import v6dirs
+import v6fix
+
+
+def segment(s, previous=None, revised=False):
+    sec = s["id"]
+    links = ([{"from": f"{previous}-1", "to": f"{sec}-1", "rel": "提供理由"}]
+             if previous else [])
+    # A forward/dangling link must be removed by tidy before delivery.
+    links.append({"from": "S99-1", "to": f"{sec}-1", "rel": "说明"})
+    return {"sec": sec, "relation": "没在想", "items": [
+        {"id": f"{sec}-1", "text": "刚才的犹豫让他再等一会儿。" if revised else "他还想再等一会儿。",
+         "key": "等一会儿", "follows": False, "explains": ["B1"]}],
+        "because": links, "gaps": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "尚未说清"}]}
+
+
+def whole_answer(w, revised=False):
+    segs = draft10.segs(w)
+    # Deliberately reverse model order: assembly must use sec, not list position.
+    return {"segments": list(reversed([segment(s, segs[i - 1]["id"] if i else None, revised)
+                                       for i, s in enumerate(segs)])),
+            "changes": [{"v6": ["R1"], "now": ["S01-1"], "what": "改写",
+                         "why": "退改版" if revised else "第一版"}]}
+
+
+def check_answer(sec, needs_revision=False, radical=False):
+    result = {"items": [{"id": f"{sec}-1", "label": "ok", "why": "符合边界"}], "links": [],
+              "readable": "是", "readable_why": "读得懂", "lost": [],
+              "unexplained": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "已在 gaps 中，应当排除"}], "wordy": []}
+    if radical:
+        result.update(progress="否" if needs_revision else "是", progress_why="需要接上前段")
+    elif needs_revision:
+        result["wordy"] = [{"ids": [f"{sec}-1"], "why": "需要精简"}]
+    return result
+
+
+class FakeModel:
+    def __init__(self, base):
+        self.base = base
+        self.calls = []
+        self.overrides = {}
+        self.lock = threading.Lock()
+        self.on_call = None
+
+    def done(self, rid):
+        return (self.base / rid / "output.txt").exists()
+
+    def output(self, rid):
+        path = self.base / rid / "output.txt"
+        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if path.exists() else None
+
+    def put(self, rid, answer):
+        path = self.base / rid
+        path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        text = answer if isinstance(answer, str) else json.dumps(answer, ensure_ascii=False)
+        (path / "output.txt").write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+        return text
+
+    def answer(self, rid):
+        if rid in self.overrides:
+            return self.overrides[rid]
+        parts = rid.split("_")
+        prefix, w = parts[:2]
+        segs = draft10.segs(w)
+        if prefix in ("v6w", "v6wr"):
+            return whole_answer(w, revised=prefix == "v6wr")
+        if prefix in ("v6k", "v6sk"):
+            sec = parts[2]
+            return check_answer(sec, needs_revision=sec == segs[1]["id"], radical=prefix == "v6sk")
+        if prefix in ("v6s", "v6sr"):
+            sec = parts[2]
+            i = next(i for i, s in enumerate(segs) if s["id"] == sec)
+            return segment(segs[i], segs[i - 1]["id"] if i else None, revised=prefix == "v6sr")
+        if prefix == "v6sc":
+            # Missing the last relation exercises the specified default + error record.
+            return {"center": "犹豫仍然影响着等待。", "why": "贯穿全曲",
+                    "relations": {s["id"]: "还说不清" for s in segs[:-1]}}
+        raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected model call: {rid}")
+
+    def call(self, rid, message, model, meta):
+        with self.lock:
+            if self.done(rid):
+                raise AssertionError(f"Completed run called again: {rid}")
+            self.calls.append({"rid": rid, "message": message, "model": model, "meta": meta})
+        if self.on_call:
+            self.on_call(rid, message)
+        path = self.base / rid
+        path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        (path / "input.txt").write_text(message, encoding="utf-8")
+        (path / "meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta), encoding="utf-8")
+        return self.put(rid, self.answer(rid))
+
+
+class OfflineTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="v6dirs_test_")
+        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
+        self.base = Path(self.temp.name).resolve()
+        self.out = self.base / "evaluations" / "v6fix"
+        self.fake = FakeModel(self.base / "runs")
+        self.stack = ExitStack()
+        self.addCleanup(self.stack.close)
+        self.stdout, self.stderr = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
+        self.stack.enter_context(redirect_stdout(self.stdout))
+        self.stack.enter_context(redirect_stderr(self.stderr))
+        self.writes = []
+        # Both pathlib and builtins writes are guarded, including unchanged join.
+        def guard(original):
+            def opened(file, mode="r", *args, **kwargs):
+                if isinstance(mode, str) and any(c in mode for c in "wax+"):
+                    path = Path(file).resolve()
+                    if not path.is_relative_to(self.base):
+                        raise AssertionError(f"Write outside temporary directory: {path}")
+                    self.writes.append(path)
+                return original(file, mode, *args, **kwargs)
+            return opened
+        self.stack.enter_context(patch("io.open", guard(io.open)))
+        self.stack.enter_context(patch("builtins.open", guard(builtins.open)))
+        for obj, name, value in ((evo, "RUNS", self.fake.base), (evo, "call", self.fake.call),
+                                 (evo, "done", self.fake.done), (evo, "output", self.fake.output),
+                                 (v6fix, "OUT", self.out)):
+            self.stack.enter_context(patch.object(obj, name, value))
+        self.parallel = self.stack.enter_context(patch.object(evo, "run_parallel", wraps=evo.run_parallel))
+
+    def read(self, w, tag):
+        return json.loads((self.out / f"{w}_{tag}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+
+    def assert_graph(self, w, tag, direction):
+        data = self.read(w, tag)
+        graph = v6fix.graph_of(w, tag)
+        self.assertEqual(data["graph"], graph)
+        self.assertEqual([s["sec"] for s in graph["segments"]], [s["id"] for s in draft10.segs(w)])
+        self.assertEqual(list(data["rows"]), [s["id"] for s in draft10.segs(w)])
+        md = (self.out / f"{w}_{tag}.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertEqual(md, f"# Op.{w[2:]}｜关系图（{direction} {tag}）\n\n" + v6fix.view(graph))
+        seen = set()
+        for s in graph["segments"]:
+            here = {x["id"] for x in s["items"]}
+            self.assertTrue(all(x["from"] in seen and x["to"] in here for x in s["because"]))
+            seen |= here
+            self.assertEqual(set(data["rows"][s["sec"]]), {"edit", "check", "notes", "revised"})
+        # The exact assembly used by blind: old/new graph -> view -> BLIND.format.
+        message = v6fix.BLIND.format(NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], WHAT=v6fix.WHAT,
+                                    X=v6fix.view(v6fix.v6(w)), Y=v6fix.view(graph), BEADS=v6fix.beads(w))
+        self.assertIn(graph["center"], message)
+        return data
+
+    def assert_routes(self):
+        for call in self.fake.calls:
+            prefix = call["rid"].split("_")[0]
+            stage = {"v6w": "v6-whole", "v6wr": "v6-whole-rev", "v6k": "v6-check",
+                     "v6s": "v6-rad", "v6sr": "v6-rad-rev", "v6sk": "v6-rad-check",
+                     "v6sc": "v6-rad-center"}[prefix]
+            writing = prefix in ("v6w", "v6wr", "v6s", "v6sr")
+            self.assertEqual(call["meta"], {"stage": stage, **({"web": True} if writing else {})})
+            fallback = call["rid"].endswith("b")
+            expected = evo.G56 if prefix in ("v6k", "v6sk") or fallback else evo.G6
+            self.assertEqual(call["model"], expected)
+
+    def test_whole_full_both_pieces_fallback_revision_cache_and_join(self):
+        self.fake.overrides["v6w_op60_w1"] = "not json"
+        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+            v6dirs.whole(w)
+            data = self.assert_graph(w, "w1", "A2 整首修补")
+            self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["center"], v6fix.v6(w)["center"])
+            self.assertTrue(all(row["revised"] for row in data["rows"].values()))
+            self.assertEqual(data["changes"][0]["why"], "退改版")
+            self.assertEqual(len(data["rows"]["S02"]["notes"]), 1)
+            self.assertEqual(data["rows"]["S01"]["notes"], [])
+            for call in self.fake.calls:
+                if call["rid"].startswith(f"v6k_{w}_"):
+                    sec = call["rid"].split("_")[2]
+                    done = call["message"].split("【前面已经修好的段】\n", 1)[1].split("\n\n【这一段 v6", 1)[0]
+                    n = int(sec[1:])
+                    for i in range(1, len(data["rows"]) + 1):
+                        self.assertEqual(f"### S{i:02d}｜" in done, i < n)
+            rev = next(x for x in self.fake.calls if x["rid"] == f"v6wr_{w}_w1")
+            note_block = rev["message"].split("【上一次的修补被核对者退回的地方】", 1)[1].split("上一次的修补结果：", 1)[0]
+            self.assertIn("### S02", note_block)
+            self.assertNotIn("### S01", note_block)
+        self.assertEqual(self.parallel.call_count, 2)
+        self.assertTrue(all(c.args[1] == 4 for c in self.parallel.call_args_list))
+        joined = v6fix.join("w1").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertIn("肖邦《船歌》Op.60", joined)
+        self.assertIn("肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61", joined)
+        self.assertIn("线没有单独说明的听感", joined)
+        before = len(self.fake.calls)
+        v6dirs.whole("op60")
+        v6dirs.whole("op61")
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.fake.calls), before)
+        self.assert_routes()
+        self.assertTrue(self.writes)
+
+    def test_rad_full_both_pieces_fallback_progress_revision_center_cache_and_join(self):
+        self.fake.overrides["v6s_op60_S01_s1"] = "not json"
+        snapshots = []
+        def inspect(rid, message):
+            if rid.startswith("v6s_") or rid.startswith("v6sc_"):
+                w = rid.split("_")[1]
+                if rid.startswith("v6sc_"):
+                    data = self.read(w, "s1")
+                    self.assertEqual(len(data["graph"]["segments"]), len(draft10.segs(w)))
+                else:
+                    sec = rid.split("_")[2]
+                    n = int(sec[1:])
+                    if n == 1:
+                        return
+                    data = self.read(w, "s1")
+                    self.assertEqual(len(data["graph"]["segments"]), n - 1)
+                    previous = data["graph"]["segments"][-1]
+                    self.assertIn(previous["items"][0]["text"], message)
+                self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["center"], "")
+                self.assertTrue(all(s["relation"] == "" for s in data["graph"]["segments"]))
+                self.assertNotIn("（没在想）", message)
+                self.assertNotIn("（正在面对）", message)
+                snapshots.append(copy.deepcopy(data))
+        self.fake.on_call = inspect
+        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+            with patch.object(v6fix, "v6", side_effect=AssertionError("B1 must not load v6")):
+                v6dirs.rad(w)
+            data = self.assert_graph(w, "s1", "B1 激进逐段")
+            self.assertTrue(data["rows"]["S02"]["revised"])
+            self.assertEqual(data["rows"]["S02"]["notes"], ["- 递进不够：需要接上前段"])
+            self.assertFalse(data["rows"]["S01"]["revised"])
+            self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["segments"][-1]["relation"], "正在面对")
+            self.assertTrue(any("Center 缺少 relation" in x for x in data["errors"]))
+            center_call = next(c for c in self.fake.calls if c["rid"] == f"v6sc_{w}_s1")
+            self.assertNotRegex(center_call["message"], r"\[S\d+-\d+\]")
+            order = [c["rid"] for c in self.fake.calls]
+            self.assertLess(order.index(f"v6sr_{w}_S02_s1"), order.index(f"v6s_{w}_S03_s1"))
+        self.assertEqual(len(snapshots), 27)
+        self.assertIn("犹豫仍然影响着等待。", v6fix.join("s1").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        self.fake.on_call = None
+        before = len(self.fake.calls)
+        v6dirs.rad("op60")
+        v6dirs.rad("op61")
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.fake.calls), before)
+        self.assert_routes()
+
+    def test_whole_no_notes_no_revision(self):
+        for s in draft10.segs("op60"):
+            self.fake.overrides[f"v6k_op60_{s['id']}_clean"] = check_answer(s["id"])
+        v6dirs.whole("op60", "clean")

+        self.assertFalse(any(r["revised"] for r in self.read("op60", "clean")["rows"].values()))
+
+    def test_whole_missing_segment_revision_failure_preserves_first(self):

+        self.assertFalse(any(r["revised"] for r in data["rows"].values()))
+        self.assertEqual(data["changes"], first["changes"])
+        self.assertEqual(len(data["errors"]), 2)

+    def test_generation_both_parse_failures_stop(self):
+        for direction, rid in (("whole", "v6w_op60_bad"), ("rad", "v6s_op60_S01_bad")):
+            self.fake.overrides.update({rid: "bad", rid + "b": "bad"})
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "G6 和 G56"):
+                getattr(v6dirs, direction)("op60", "bad")
+        self.assertFalse(self.out.exists())
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.fake.calls), 4)
+        self.assert_routes()
+
+    def test_rad_revision_failure_and_center_failure_preserve_progress(self):
+        self.fake.overrides.update(v6sr_op60_S02_failcenter="bad", v6sc_op60_failcenter="bad")
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "Center"):
+            v6dirs.rad("op60", "failcenter")
+        data = self.read("op60", "failcenter")
+        self.assertEqual(len(data["graph"]["segments"]), 14)
+        self.assertEqual(data["graph"]["center"], "")
+        self.assertFalse(data["rows"]["S02"]["revised"])
+        self.assertIn("他还想再等一会儿。", v6fix.view(data["graph"]))
+        self.assertTrue(any("逐段退改解析失败" in x for x in data["errors"]))
+        self.assertTrue((self.out / "op60_failcenter.md").exists())
+
+    def test_check_failure_stops_without_accepting_unchecked_output(self):
+        self.fake.overrides["v6sk_op60_S02_checkfail"] = "bad"
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "核对"):
+            v6dirs.rad("op60", "checkfail")
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.read("op60", "checkfail")["graph"]["segments"]), 1)

+        self.fake.overrides["v6k_op60_S01_checkfailwhole"] = "bad"
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "核对"):
+            v6dirs.whole("op60", "checkfailwhole")
+        self.assertFalse((self.out / "op60_checkfailwhole.json").exists())
+
+    def test_center_relations_entirely_missing_use_recorded_defaults(self):
+        self.fake.overrides["v6sc_op61_norelations"] = {"center": "等待和犹豫一直有关。"}
+        v6dirs.rad("op61", "norelations")
+        data = self.read("op61", "norelations")
+        self.assertTrue(all(s["relation"] == "正在面对" for s in data["graph"]["segments"]))
+        self.assertEqual(len(data["errors"]), 13)

+    def test_rad_review_criteria_without_lost(self):
+        k = {"items": [{"id": "S02-1", "label": "越界", "why": "新增事件"}],
+             "links": [{"from": "S01-1", "to": "S02-1", "label": "勉强", "why": "不能推出"}],
+             "readable": "否", "readable_why": "难懂", "lost": [{"v6": ["R1"], "what": "不应出现"}],
+             "unexplained": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "已在 gaps"}, {"bead": "B5", "why": "未解释"}],
+             "wordy": [{"ids": ["S02-1"], "why": "重复"}], "progress": "否", "progress_why": "没有前因"}
+        edit = {"gaps": [{"bead": "B4", "why": "尚未解释"}]}
+        notes = v6dirs.rad_notes(k, {}, edit)
+        expected = v6fix.review_notes({**k, "lost": []}, {}, edit) + ["- 递进不够：没有前因"]
+        self.assertEqual(notes, expected)
+        self.assertEqual(len(notes), 6)
+        self.assertFalse(any("丢了 v6" in n or "B4" in n for n in notes))
+
+    def test_dry_offline_and_placeholders(self):
+        with patch.object(evo, "ROOT", self.base):
+            for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+                messages = v6dirs.dry(w)
+                self.assertEqual(len(messages), 5)
+                for name, message in messages.items():
+                    self.assertEqual(message, (self.base / "_codex" / f"dry_{w}_{name}.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+                    self.assertNotRegex(message, r"\{[A-Z_]+\}")
+                    self.assertNotIn("{{", message)
+                    # Nested JSON objects legitimately end in }}; parse the
+                    # sample instead of treating closing braces as placeholders.
+                    blocks = re.findall(r"```json\s*(.*?)```", message, re.S)
+                    self.assertEqual(len(blocks), 1)
+                    json.loads(blocks[0])
+                self.assertIn(v6dirs.FIRST, messages["rad_first"])
+                self.assertIn("[S01-1]", messages["rad_second"])
+                self.assertNotIn("（正在面对）", messages["rad_second"])
+        self.assertEqual(self.fake.calls, [])
+
+    def test_reserved_tag_and_path_escape_do_not_call_or_write(self):
+        for fn in (v6dirs.whole, v6dirs.rad):
+            for tag in ("r2", "../r2", "..\\r2", "", "a/b"):
+                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                    fn("op60", tag)
+        self.assertEqual(self.fake.calls, [])
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+
+class BaselineTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.baseline = json.loads((ROOT / "_codex" / "v6dirs_baseline.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+
+    def test_protected_files_and_all_v6fix_code_except_view_unchanged(self):
+        for name, digest in self.baseline["sha256"].items():
+            self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256((ROOT / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest(), digest, name)
+        source = (ROOT / "v6fix.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        tree = ast.parse(source)
+        stripped = ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if not (isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == "view")], type_ignores=[])
+        self.assertEqual(ast.dump(stripped, include_attributes=False), self.baseline["v6fix_without_view_ast"])
+        original = '            md += [f"### {s[\'sec\']}｜{s[\'bars\']} 小节　（{s.get(\'relation\')}）", ""]'
+        changed = ('            relation = f"　（{s.get(\'relation\')}）" if s.get("relation") else ""\n'
+                   '            md += [f"### {s[\'sec\']}｜{s[\'bars\']} 小节{relation}", ""]')
+        expected = self.baseline["view_source"].replace(original, changed)
+        self.assertNotEqual(expected, self.baseline["view_source"])
+        self.assertEqual(ast.get_source_segment(source, next(n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == "view")), expected)
+
+    def test_both_real_v6_views_exactly_match_before_change(self):
+        namespace = dict(vars(v6fix))
+        exec(self.baseline["view_source"], namespace)
+        old_view = namespace["view"]
+        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
+            graph = v6fix.v6(w)
+            self.assertTrue(all(s.get("relation") for s in graph["segments"]))
+            for ids in (False, True):
+                self.assertEqual(v6fix.view(graph, ids=ids), self.baseline["views"][w][str(ids)])
+            for ids, center, head, secs in itertools.product((False, True), (False, True), (False, True), (None, ["S02", "S03"])):
+                opts = dict(ids=ids, center=center, head=head, secs=secs)
+                self.assertEqual(v6fix.view(graph, **opts), old_view(graph, **opts), (w, opts))
+        for relation in (None, ""):
+            graph = {"center": "", "segments": [{"sec": "S01", "bars": "1–3", "relation": relation, "items": [], "because": []}]}
+            self.assertEqual(v6fix.view(graph, center=False), "### S01｜1–3 小节")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

+# v6dirs 实现与验证记录
+
+工作目录：`D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019`。任务日期：2026-10-29。
+
+## 边界与准备
+
+- 只实现 A2/B1 运行器和离线验证；未启动真实 `whole` / `rad`。
+- Python 读写文本全部显式使用 UTF-8。最初尝试 PowerShell here-string 经管道传给 Python 时，PowerShell 5.1 在 stdin 前带了 BOM，触发 SyntaxError；该命令未执行任何文件操作。随后改用 `python -c` 和补丁工具。
+- 已阅读 `v6fix.py`、`v6dirs_prompts.py`、`evo.py` 及分段、作品名与珠子读取接口；工作目录及检查的父目录没有适用的 AGENTS.md。
+- 修改前保存 `_codex/v6dirs_baseline.json`：受保护文件 SHA-256、除 view 外的 v6fix AST、原 view 函数源码、两首 v6 在 ids=False/True 下的原始渲染。
+- op60 有 14 段，op61 有 13 段。
+- 不操作后台进程、watchdog、缓存保温、定时任务。运行器拒绝使用 `r2`，避免写入仍在运行的 A1 结果。
+
+## 实现
+
+- 新增 `v6dirs.py`，提供 `whole <w> [w1]`、`rad <w> [s1]`、`dry <w>`。
+- A2：原始 v6 + 全曲珠子 + 顺序诊断；按 sec 重组；tidy；核对使用独立的前缀图，调用原 `v6fix.check`，`evo.run_parallel(..., 4)`；收集原 `review_notes`，最多一次整首退改；保留最终 edit 和 changes。
+- B1：按段串行；只传已完成段落和本段珠子；首次和退改后均清空 relation；核对沿用 A1 的意见提取规则，仅去掉 lost 并增加 progress；每段立即保存 JSON/MD；最后调用 Center 并填 relation，缺项默认“正在面对”且记录。
+- 首轮生成 JSON/基本容器结构无法解析时，使用 G56 和后缀 b 重试一次；所有调用先检查 `evo.done`。退改失败保留第一版并记录。核对或 Center 无有效解析结果时抛错退出，B1 保留已落盘进度。
+- 所有模型调用仅经 `evo.call`，写作/退改 meta 有 web=True；核对/Center 没有 web。没有额外模型参数、推理参数、槽位、节流、轮询。
+- JSON 保持 graph/rows 接口；A2 另含 changes；异常兜底情况另存 errors。MD 使用指定 A2/B1 标题及 `v6fix.view(graph)`。
+- `v6fix.py` 仅修改 view 的段标题两行：relation 为 None 或空串时省略括号，非空时保留原完整空格、标点和内容。
+- `_codex/test_v6dirs.py` 使用 unittest/monkeypatch；FakeModel 的 call/done/output 仅使用 TemporaryDirectory，另将 evo.RUNS 和 v6fix.OUT 指向临时目录。文件写入守卫同时检查 builtins.open 与 io.open，任何临时目录外写入立即报错。
+
+## 初次 dry
+
+直接运行 `python v6dirs.py dry op60` 和 `python v6dirs.py dry op61`，均成功，输出到 `_codex/dry_{w}_*.txt`。
+
+| 消息 | op60 字符数 | op61 字符数 |
+| --- | ---: | ---: |
+| whole | 51279 | 52314 |
+| rad_first | 2510 | 2813 |
+| rad_second | 2752 | 2904 |
+| rad_check | 1572 | 1717 |
+| rad_center | 460 | 467 |
+
+以上为 Python len，包含空格与换行。whole 的诊断来自现有只读诊断缓存；dry 中 B1 使用明确构造的假段落，不调用模型。
+
+## 验证结果
+
+首轮离线执行：12 项中 11 项通过；唯一失败是测试错误地把 Center 示例中嵌套 JSON 的合法 `}}` 当成未展开花括号。运行器和提示词没有问题。已删除错误的括号尾部断言，保留大写占位符检查与对每份 JSON 示例的 `json.loads` 验证。B1 完整流程额外禁止调用 v6fix.v6，确认运行器不会读取底稿。
+
+待补充修正后的完整测试及最终边界自查。

+"""A2 whole-piece repair and B1 chained generation; prompts owned by v6dirs_prompts.
+
+python v6dirs.py whole <op60|op61> [tag=w1]
+python v6dirs.py rad   <op60|op61> [tag=s1]
+python v6dirs.py dry   <op60|op61>       # no model calls
+"""
+import argparse
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+
+import covjudge
+import draft10
+import evo
+import v6dirs_prompts as prompts
+import v6fix
+
+
+FIRST = "（这是第一段，前面还没有）"
+
+
+def _target(w, tag):
+    if w not in covjudge.NAME:
+        raise ValueError(f"未知作品：{w}")
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]*", tag):
+        raise ValueError("tag 只能包含英文字母、数字、下划线和连字符")
+    if tag == "r2":
+        raise ValueError("r2 属于正在运行的 A1，不能覆盖；请使用独立 tag")
+
+
+def _record(errors, text):
+    errors.append(text)
+    print(f"WARNING: {text}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
+
+
+def _ask(rid, message, model, stage, web=False):
+    meta = {"stage": stage}
+    if web:
+        meta["web"] = True
+    if not evo.done(rid):
+        evo.call(rid, message, model=model, meta=meta)
+    return v6fix.parse(rid)
+
+
+def _whole_ok(result):
+    return isinstance(result, dict) and isinstance(result.get("segments"), list)
+
+
+def _segment_ok(result):
+    return isinstance(result, dict) and isinstance(result.get("items"), list)
+
+
+def _generate(rid, message, stage, valid):
+    for run_id, model in ((rid, evo.G6), (rid + "b", evo.G56)):
+        result = _ask(run_id, message, model, stage, web=True)
+        if valid(result):
+            return result
+    raise RuntimeError(f"{rid}: G6 和 G56 均未返回可解析的结果，停")
+
+
+def whole_message(w, g6=None):
+    if g6 is None:
+        g6 = v6fix.v6(w)
+    diag = "\n\n".join(f"### {s['id']}\n" + v6fix.diag_for(w, s["id"])
+                       for s in draft10.segs(w))
+    return prompts.WHOLE.format(
+        NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], WHAT=v6fix.WHAT, CENTER=g6["center"],
+        V6=v6fix.view(g6, ids=True, center=False), BEADS=v6fix.beads(w), DIAG=diag)
+
+
+def _assemble(w, result, center, errors):
+    by_sec = {x.get("sec"): x for x in result["segments"] if isinstance(x, dict)}
+    graph = {"center": center, "segments": []}
+    edits = {}
+    for s in draft10.segs(w):
+        sec = s["id"]
+        edit = by_sec.get(sec)
+        if edit is None:
+            _record(errors, f"{w} {sec}: 整首输出缺段，使用空 items")
+            edit = {"sec": sec, "items": [], "because": [], "gaps": []}
+        edits[sec] = edit
+        graph["segments"].append(v6fix.seg_graph(edit, sec, s))
+    return v6fix.tidy(graph), edits
+
+
+def _save(w, tag, graph, rows, direction, errors, **extra):
+    v6fix.OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    data = {"graph": graph, "rows": rows, **extra}
+    if errors:
+        data["errors"] = list(errors)
+    (v6fix.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.json").write_text(
+        json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
+    opus = "Op.60" if w == "op60" else "Op.61"
+    (v6fix.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.md").write_text(
+        f"# {opus}｜关系图（{direction} {tag}）\n\n" + v6fix.view(graph), encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def whole(w, tag="w1"):
+    _target(w, tag)
+    g6 = v6fix.v6(w)
+    message = whole_message(w, g6)
+    errors = []
+    result = _generate(f"v6w_{w}_{tag}", message, "v6-whole", _whole_ok)
+    graph, edits = _assemble(w, result, g6["center"], errors)
+    jobs = []
+    for i, new in enumerate(graph["segments"]):
+        sec = new["sec"]
+        done = {"center": g6["center"], "segments": graph["segments"][:i]}
+        cur = {"center": g6["center"], "segments": [x for x in g6["segments"] if x["sec"] == sec]}
+        jobs.append((sec, lambda sec=sec, done=done, cur=cur, new=new:
+                     v6fix.check(w, sec, tag, done, cur, new)))
+    checks = evo.run_parallel(jobs, 4)
+    rows = {}
+    for new in graph["segments"]:
+        sec = new["sec"]
+        k = checks.get(sec)
+        if not isinstance(k, dict) or not k:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"{w} {sec}: 核对未返回可解析的结果，停")
+        rows[sec] = {"edit": edits[sec], "check": k,
+                     "notes": v6fix.review_notes(k, new, edits[sec]), "revised": False}
+    if any(row["notes"] for row in rows.values()):
+        notes = "\n\n".join(f"### {sec}\n" + "\n".join(row["notes"])
+                            for sec, row in rows.items() if row["notes"])
+        revision = _ask(
+            f"v6wr_{w}_{tag}", message + prompts.WHOLE_REV.format(
+                NOTES=notes, PREV=v6fix.view(graph, ids=True, center=False)),
+            evo.G6, "v6-whole-rev", web=True)
+        if _whole_ok(revision):
+            result = revision
+            graph, edits = _assemble(w, result, g6["center"], errors)
+            for sec, row in rows.items():
+                row.update(edit=edits[sec], revised=True)
+        else:
+            _record(errors, f"{w}: 整首退改解析失败，保留第一版")
+    _save(w, tag, graph, rows, "A2 整首修补", errors, changes=result.get("changes") or [])
+    old = {x["sec"]: x for x in g6["segments"]}
+    for new in graph["segments"]:
+        sec = new["sec"]
+        row = rows[sec]
+        print(f"{w} {sec}: v6 {len(old[sec]['items'])} 条 → {len(new['items'])} 条"
+              f" | 意见 {len(row['notes'])} | 退改 {row['revised']}", flush=True)
+    return graph
+
+
+def _done(graph):
+    return v6fix.view(graph, ids=True, center=False) if graph["segments"] else FIRST
+
+
+def rad_message(w, s, graph):
+    segs = "\n".join(f"{x['id']}｜{x['start']}–{x['end']} 小节" for x in draft10.segs(w))
+    return prompts.RAD.format(NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], SEC=s["id"], A=s["start"], B=s["end"],
+                              WHAT=prompts.WHAT_RAD, SEGS=segs, DONE=_done(graph),
+                              BEADS=v6fix.beads(w, s["id"]))
+
+
+def rad_check_message(w, sec, graph, new):
+    return prompts.RAD_CHK.format(
+        DONE=_done(graph), NEW=v6fix.view({"center": "", "segments": [new]}, ids=True, center=False)
+        + v6fix._links(new), BEADS=v6fix.beads(w, sec))
+
+
+def rad_center_message(w, graph):
+    return prompts.RAD_CENTER.format(
+        NAME=covjudge.NAME[w], LINE=v6fix.view(graph, ids=False, center=False))
+
+
+def rad_notes(k, new, edit):
+    # Reuse exactly the A1 criteria, except B1 has progression instead of lost-v6.
+    notes = v6fix.review_notes({key: value for key, value in k.items() if key != "lost"}, new, edit)
+    if k.get("progress") == "否":
+        notes.append(f"- 递进不够：{k.get('progress_why', '')}")
+    return notes
+
+
+def _rad_segment(edit, s):
+    new = v6fix.seg_graph(edit, s["id"], s)
+    new["relation"] = ""
+    return new
+
+
+def rad(w, tag="s1"):
+    _target(w, tag)
+    graph = {"center": "", "segments": []}
+    rows, errors = {}, []
+    for s in draft10.segs(w):
+        sec = s["id"]
+        message = rad_message(w, s, graph)
+        edit = _generate(f"v6s_{w}_{sec}_{tag}", message, "v6-rad", _segment_ok)
+        new = _rad_segment(edit, s)
+        k = _ask(f"v6sk_{w}_{sec}_{tag}", rad_check_message(w, sec, graph, new),
+                 evo.G56, "v6-rad-check")
+        if not isinstance(k, dict) or not k:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"{w} {sec}: 核对未返回可解析的结果，停；已完成的段保留在进度文件")
+        notes = rad_notes(k, new, edit)
+        revised = False
+        if notes:
+            prev = v6fix.view({"center": "", "segments": [new]}, ids=True, center=False)
+            revision = _ask(f"v6sr_{w}_{sec}_{tag}",
+                            message + prompts.RAD_REV.format(NOTES="\n".join(notes), PREV=prev),
+                            evo.G6, "v6-rad-rev", web=True)
+            if _segment_ok(revision):
+                edit, new, revised = revision, _rad_segment(revision, s), True
+            else:
+                _record(errors, f"{w} {sec}: 逐段退改解析失败，保留第一版")
+        graph["segments"].append(new)
+        v6fix.tidy(graph)
+        rows[sec] = {"edit": edit, "check": k, "notes": notes, "revised": revised}
+        _save(w, tag, graph, rows, "B1 激进逐段", errors)
+        print(f"{w} {sec}: {len(new['items'])} 条 | 意见 {len(notes)} | 退改 {revised}"
+              f" | gaps {[x.get('bead') for x in edit.get('gaps') or []]}", flush=True)
+    result = _ask(f"v6sc_{w}_{tag}", rad_center_message(w, graph), evo.G6, "v6-rad-center")
+    if not (isinstance(result, dict) and isinstance(result.get("center"), str)
+            and result["center"].strip()
+            and (result.get("relations") is None or isinstance(result["relations"], dict))):
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{w}: Center 未返回可解析的结果，停；逐段进度已保存")
+    graph["center"] = result["center"]
+    relations = result.get("relations") or {}
+    for seg in graph["segments"]:
+        sec = seg["sec"]
+        relation = relations.get(sec)
+        if not relation:
+            relation = "正在面对"
+            _record(errors, f"{w} {sec}: Center 缺少 relation，填入正在面对")
+        seg["relation"] = relation
+    _save(w, tag, graph, rows, "B1 激进逐段", errors)
+    return graph
+
+
+def dry(w):
+    segs = draft10.segs(w)
+    graph = {"center": "", "segments": []}
+    first = _rad_segment({"items": [{"id": f"{segs[0]['id']}-1", "text": "他还说不清自己的感受。",
+                                    "key": "说不清", "explains": ["B1"]}], "because": [], "gaps": []}, segs[0])
+    fake = {"center": "", "segments": [first]}
+    second = _rad_segment({"items": [{"id": f"{segs[1]['id']}-1", "text": "刚才的犹豫让他多想了一会儿。",
+                                     "key": "犹豫", "explains": ["B1"]}],
+                           "because": [{"from": first["items"][0]["id"], "to": f"{segs[1]['id']}-1",
+                                        "rel": "提供理由"}]}, segs[1])
+    messages = {"whole": whole_message(w), "rad_first": rad_message(w, segs[0], graph),
+                "rad_second": rad_message(w, segs[1], fake),
+                "rad_check": rad_check_message(w, segs[1]["id"], fake, second),
+                "rad_center": rad_center_message(w, {"center": "", "segments": [first, second]})}
+    out = evo.ROOT / "_codex"
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for name, message in messages.items():
+        path = out / f"dry_{w}_{name}.txt"
+        path.write_text(message, encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"{path.name}: {len(message)} 字符", flush=True)
+    return messages
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    parser.add_argument("direction", choices=("whole", "rad", "dry"))
+    parser.add_argument("work", choices=("op60", "op61"))
+    parser.add_argument("tag", nargs="?")
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        if args.direction == "dry":
+            if args.tag is not None:
+                parser.error("dry 不接受 tag")
+            dry(args.work)
+        elif args.direction == "whole":
+            whole(args.work, args.tag or "w1")
+        else:
+            rad(args.work, args.tag or "s1")
+    except (RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
+        parser.exit(1, f"ERROR: {exc}\n")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
